patippurai
tamiḻukkum tamiḻarukkum vaḷamum valimaiyum cērkkak
kūṭiya paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷaiyellām tēṭiyeṭuttut tamiḻ kūṟum ulakiṟku vaḻaṅkum tolainōkkup pārvaiyuṭaṉ  tamiḻmaṇ patippakam toṭaṅkappaṭṭatu. ataṉ vāyilāka moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇariṉ nūṟṟāṇṭu niṉaivāka avar eḻutiya nūlkaḷ aṉaittaiyum cērttu avaruṭaiya maṟaivukkup piṟaku 2000-ttilum, palvēṟu itaḻkaḷilum, malarkaḷilum veḷivantu, nūl vaṭivam peṟāta pāvāṇariṉ ariya kaṭṭuraikaḷai ellām tokuttu 2001- lum orucēra veḷiyiṭṭu ulakeṅkum uḷḷa tamiḻarkaḷukku vaḻaṅkiṉōm.  pāvāṇar vaḻi nilai aṟiñarkaḷāṉa mutumuṉaivar 
irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉār avarkaḷum, maruttuvar ku.pūṅkāvaṉam avarkaḷum ivvariya kaṭṭuraip putaiyalkaḷ nūl vaṭivam peṟuvataṟku mikavum uṟutuṇaiyāka iruntaṉar. ipperumakkaḷai naṉṟiyuṇarvuṭaṉ niṉaivukūrkiṟēṉ.
collārāyccit tuṟaiyil tēvanēyar oppaṟṟa taṉit
tiṟamaiyuṭaivar eṉṟu maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷum, nāvalan tīvukku  nantamiḻē tāyeṉṟu pāvēntarum, tamiḻukkum tamiḻ
nāṭṭukkum naṟṟoṇṭu āṟṟiyavar pāvāṇar eṉṟu pēraṟiñar aṇṇā avarkaḷum, veṭṭa veṭṭak kiṭaikkum taṅkac
curaṅkam pōṉṟavar eṉṟu tamiḻiṟiñar irācamāṇikkaṉārum, tamiḻakam moḻittuṟaiyilē pāvāṇar pōṉṟa oru aṟiñarai iṉṉum peṟṟut taravillai eṉṟu paṉmoḻip pulavar
appātturaiyarum, kuṟaimatiyar tēkkivaitta karaiyiruḷai nīkka vanta maṟaimalaiyār vaḻivanta niṟaimalaiyār pāvāṇar eṉṟu mēṉāḷ pēravaittalaivar tamiḻkuṭimakaṉ avarkaḷum, tamiḻar yār? etirikaḷ yār? eṉṟu āyntu aṟintu kāṭṭiyavar pāvāṇar eṉṟu pērāciriyar iḷavaracu avarkaḷum,  ōrāyiram āṇṭukaḷ ōyntu kiṭantapiṉ vārātu pōla vanta māmaṇi pāvāṇar eṉṟu mutumuṉaivar iḷaṅkumaraṉār avarkaḷum kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷa  perumaikaḷukkuriya pēraṟiñariṉ nūlkaḷai mīḷpatippaka veḷiyiṭuvatil perumai koḷkiṟēṉ.
pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ attaṉaiyum tamiḻmoḻikku ēṟṟam uraippaṉa. tamiḻai āriya iruḷiṉiṉṟu mīṭṭuk kāppaṉa. vīḻntu
paṭṭa tamiḻaṉukku viḻippūṭṭa vallaṉa. āyiramāyiram āṇṭukaḷāka aṭimaippaṭṭukkiṭakkum tamiḻmoḻiyai mīṭkavallatu eṉṟu  pāvalarēṟu peruñcittiraṉār avarkaḷiṉ kūṟṟai ik kaḷañciya veḷiyīṭṭil pativu ceyvatu ematu kaṭamaiyākum.
pāvāṇarait tūkkip piṭittāltāṉ tamiḻiṉam urup
paṭamuṭiyum - uyaramuṭiyum. pāvāṇar koḷkaikaḷ tamiḻar uḷḷamellām nilaittu niṟpataṟkum, pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ tamiḻar illamelām iṭam peṟuvataṟkum muṉ kuṟippiṭṭa 2000 - 2001 kālakkaṭṭattil veḷiyiṭappaṭṭa pāvāṇariṉ aṟivuk karuvūlaṅkaḷaiyellām oṭṭu mottamāka cērttu orē vīccil pāvāṇar tamiḻk kaḷañciyam eṉum talaippil  em aṟakkaṭṭaḷai veḷiyiṭukiṟatu.
maṟaikkappeṟṟa māperum varalāṟṟaiyum, citaikkap peṟṟa oppuyarvuyaṟṟa moḻiyaiyum koṇṭa tamiḻiṉattiṉ muṉṉēṟṟam karuti ivvaruntamiḻ putaiyalkaḷai tamiḻ kūṟum nallulakiṟku  vaḻaṅka muṉ vantuḷḷōm. tamiḻ moḻiyai mūccākavum, pēccākavum, ceyalākavum koṇṭu orunāḷiṉ muḻuppoḻutum tamiḻākavē vāḻnta cempulaccemmal,  taṉittamiḻk katiravaṉ moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇar nūlkaḷai vāṅkip payaṉ koḷvīr.
iḷamaiyilēyē potuttoṇṭilum, taṉittamiḻ iyakkat toṇṭilum nāṉ īṭupāṭu koḷvataṟkuk kāraṇamāka iruntavarkaḷ perumpulavar nakkīraṉ avarkaḷum, avartam iḷaval pulavar cittiravēlaṉ avarkaḷum āvar. ivarkaḷai ivveḷiyiṭṭiṉ vāyilāka naṉṟiyuṇarvōṭu niṉaivu kūṟukiṟēṉ.  tantai periyāriṉ taṉmatippu iyakkak koḷkai
kaḷālum, moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇariṉ taṉittamiḻ iyakkak koḷkaikaḷālum īrkkappaṭṭavaṉ. attaku piṉpulattōṭu patippuppaṇiyil eṉ kālaṭic cuvaṭukaḷaip patittu varukiṟēṉ.
kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ,
patippāḷar.
vāṉmaḻai vaḷaccurappu
āḻattuḷ āḻamāy akaḻntu taṅkam ēṉ eṭukkappaṭukiṟatu? āḻkaṭaluḷ ceṉṟu, uyiraip paṇayam vaittu muttuk kuḷikkap paṭuvatu ēṉ? pavaḻap pāḷaṅkaḷai eṭuppatu ēṉ? vayiram mutalām maṇikkulaṅkaḷai iṭaiyaṟāmal tēṭit tēṭi eṭuppatu ēṉ?
aṇikalap poruḷkaḷ eṉpataṟku maṭṭumā? vīṭṭiṟku maṭṭumaṉṟi nāṭṭukkum īṭṭutaṟku ariya vaippu vaḷamāka iruntu, nāṭṭiṉ poruḷiyal mēmpāṭṭukku aṭimaṇai
yākavum ivai iruppatāl tāṉē!
ivaṟṟiṉum mēlām vaippu vaḷamum uṇṭō? uṇṭu! uṇṭu! avai cāṉṟōr nūlkaḷ!
kālam kālamāka aḷḷik koṇṭālum vaṟṟā vaḷamāyt tikaḻum aṟivuk karuvūlamām nūlkaḷ!
oṉṟaik kaṟṟāl oru nūṟāy ōrāyiramāyp palkip perukat takka peṟaṟkariya tāy nūlkaḷām pēṟamainta nūlkaḷ! cēynūlkaḷai īṉavalla ceḻu nūlkaḷ!
nuṇ māṇ nuḻaipulat tāy nūlkaḷāy iruppiṉum! avaṟṟait tāṅkiya tāḷkaḷ, ettakaiya pēṇutal uṭaiyavai eṉiṉum kāla veḷḷattil aḻiyak kūṭiyavai tāmē!
kalleḻuttē, katir veppālum kaṭaluppālum kāttup pēṇum kaṭappāṭillār kaippaṭalālum aḻintu paṭṭamai kaṇkūṭu tāṉē!
ēṭṭu varaivukaḷ, nīrē neruppē nīṭitta kālamē puṟṟē pōṟṟā maṭamaiyē eṉpavaṟṟāl aḻintu paṭṭamaikku aḷavu tāṉum uṇṭō? makkaḷukku māṇap periya payaṉām nūlkaḷ, kaṟkum makkaḷ perukkattiṟkum, piṟantu perukivarum makkaḷ perukkattiṟkum takka vakaiyil avarkaḷukkup pāṭṭaṉ pāṭṭiyar vaitta paḻantēṭṭeṉṉa annūlkaḷ, mīḷamīḷat taṭṭillāmal kiṭaikkac ceyya vēṇṭuvatu nūṟpatippar tavirāk kaṭamaiyallavō!
akkaṭamaiyai avarkaḷ kālantōṟum kaṭappāṭākak koṇṭu ceyya, āḷum aracum vāḻum aṟivarum arunnuṇaiyātal tāṉē kaṭṭāyat tēvai! ivvāṟu patipparum aṟivarum āḷnarum tattam mūccuk kaṭaṉāka, nūṟkoṭai puritalukku mēṟkoṭai illaiyām!
moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ oṉṟā iraṇṭā? namakku eṭṭātavai pōka eṭṭiya nūlkaḷ 32. kaṭṭurai poḻivu mutalām vakaiyāl kiṭṭiya tiraṭṭu nūlkaḷ 12. iṉṉum eṭṭāta tokuppum aṅkoṉṟum iṅkoṉṟum vara vāyppuṇṭu!
kaṭita varaivu pā varaivu eṉa mēlum perukavum vāyppuṇṭu!
ivaṟṟai ellām āyiram acciṭṭu avvaḷavil niṉṟu viṭṭāl, tēṭuvārkkut tēṭum poḻutil vāykkum tiruvākat tikaḻak kūṭumō?
ātalāl, cēy nūlkaḷukku mūlamām tāynūlkaḷ, kālam tōṟum vāṉmaḻai vaḷac curappāka veḷippaṭa vēṇṭum kaṭṭāyam uṇarntu kaṭamai puriyum vīṟuṭaiyār vēṇṭum! mika vēṇṭum!
ittakaiya viḻumiya vīṟuṭaiyar - iṉamāṉac cemmal - tamiḻp pōrāḷi - tiruttaku kō. iḷavaḻakaṉār, tām muntu muḻutuṟak koṇarnta pāvāṇar paṭaippukaḷai mīḷamīḷat tamiḻulāk koḷḷa vaikkum muṉ muyaṟciyāy, im mīḷpatippai vaḻaṅkukiṟār! tamiḻulakam payaṉ koḷvatāka! payaṉ ceyvatāka!
vāḻiya nalaṉē! vāḻiya nilaṉē!
iṉpa aṉpuṭaṉ
irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ.

paṇpāṭṭuk kaṭṭuraikaḷ


1
puṟanāṉūṟum moḻiyum
1. muṉṉurai
tamiḻaiyum ataṉ ilakkiyattaiyum cevvaiyāyaṟiyavēṇṭiṉ, tamiḻiṉ varalāṟṟaṟivu iṉṟiyamaiyātu vēṇṭappaṭum. im muṟai VṉaibmāḻikaṭkumVṟkum.giHa āṅkila ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷilellām muṉṉuraiyāka āṅkilac carittiram kūṟappaṭutalai nōkkuka. tamiḻum tamiḻarum tōṉṟiyatu māntaṉ mutaṉmutal tōṉṟiya kumarināṭē eṉpatum, kumarināṭṭār vaṭakkē cellac cellat tamiḻ mutalāvatu tiraviṭamāyum piṉpu āriyamāyun tirinta teṉpatum, āriyar tamiḻakat tiṟku varumuṉpē tamiḻar piṟa tuṟaikaḷiṟ pōṉṟu moḻiyilum talaiciṟantiruntaṉa reṉpatum, paṇṭait tamiḻnūlkaḷellām taṉit tamiḻ naṭaiyiṉavē yeṉpatuvum, piṟavum, moḻi nūlār kaṇṭa uṇmaikaḷām. tamiḻnūlkaḷai yārāyāmalē kumarināṭṭai ūkittaṟinta mēlaik kalaivallār ataṉai lemuriyā (Lemuria) eṉṟum, kāṇṭu vāṉā (Gondwana) eṉṟum aḻaittaṉar. avarkaḷ tamiḻ nūlkaḷai maṭṭum aṟintiruppiṉ, itupōtu, kumarināṭṭaippaṟṟi aiyattiṟkō maṟuppiṟkō eḷḷaḷavum iṭamiruntirukkātu.
2. tamiḻiṉ kālap pakuti
tamiḻiṉ kālam kīḻkkāṇumāṟu mupperum pakutiyiṉatākum
(1)	mutaṟkālam ki.mu. cumār 10,000 - 3,000.
(2)	iṭaikkālam allatu kaḻakakkālam - ki.mu. cumār 3,000 - ki.pi. 200.
(3)	taṟkālam - ki.pi. 200 mutal.
ivaṟṟuḷ ovvoṉṟum pala ciṟu pakutikaḷākap pākupaṭṭuḷḷatu. kaḻakak kālam (1) talaik kaḻakakkālam, (2) iṭaik kaḻakakkālam, (3) kaṭaik kaḻakakkālam eṉa muppakutippaṭum. ivaṟṟukku iṭaiyiṭai iṭaiyīṭu miruntatu. iṟaiyaṉā rakapporuḷuraippaṭi, mukkaḻakamum nilaittirunta kālam muṟaiyē nālāyirattu nāṉūṟṟu nāṟpatiṟṟi yāṇṭum mūvāyirat teḻunūṟṟi yāṇṭum āyirat teṇṇūṟṟaimpatiṟṟi yāṇṭumāka mottam oṉpatiṉāyirattut toḷāyirattut toṇṇūṟi yāṇṭākum. kumarināṭṭiṉ toṉmutu paḻaimaiyai nōkkuvārkkuk kaḻakakkāla neṭumaiyaip paṟṟiya aiyuṟavum mayakkamum aṟavē aṟumeṉpatu tiṇṇam.
puṟanāṉūṟṟil oru cila (2, 358, 362, 366) ceyyuḷkaḷait tavira, piṟavaṟṟaiyellām pāṭiṉārum avarāṟ pāṭappaṭṭārum kaṭaikkaḻakak kālattiṉarātaliṉ, puṟanāṉūṟṟiṉ kālam kaṭaikkaḻakak kālamākum (ki.pi. 2ām nūṟṟāṇṭu).
3. nūlvakai
talaik kaḻakattārum iṭaik kaḻakattārum iyaṟṟiṉa nūṟṟuk kaṇakkāṉa pala tuṟait taṉittamiḻ nūlkaḷellām iṟantupaṭṭaṉa. itupōtu kaḻaka nūlkaḷeṉṟu kuṟikkappaṭupavai kaṭaikkaḻaka nūlkaḷē. avaṟṟuḷḷum, ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷ katai nūlkaḷ kalai nūlkaḷ mutaliyaṉa cērkkappaṭavillai. patiṉeṇ mēṟkaṇakkeṉṟum patiṉeṇ kīḻkkaṇak keṉṟum irupāṟpaṭṭuk kaṭaikkaḻaka nūlkaḷeṉṟu vaḻaṅkum muppat tāṟum, perumpālum, kātal vāḻkkai, maṟam, pōr, araca vāḻttu, nal loḻukkam, varalāṟṟut tuṇukku ākiya āṟuporuḷ paṟṟiyavē. mēṟ kaṇakkil, eṭṭu nūlkaḷē tokai nūlkaḷeṉap pirittuk kūṟappaṭiṉum, uṇmaiyil ellām tokai nūlē. eṭṭuttokai pōṉṟē pattup pāṭṭum pala pulavarāṟ pāṭappaṭṭatākum. pattuppāṭṭiluḷḷa pāṭṭuka ḷellām mika nīṇṭiruttalālēyē vēṟākat tokukkappaṭṭaṉa. aṭiyiṉ ciṟappē pāṭṭeṉap paṭumē eṉṟu tolkāppiyaṅ kūṟu vatāl, nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa aṭikaḷ koṇṭu mika nīṇṭirunta taṉic ceyyuḷai (perumpālum akavalai)p pāṭṭeṉṟu ciṟappāka muṉṉōr vaḻaṅkiyatākat terikiṉṟatu.
patiṉeṇ kīḻkkaṇakki ṉuḷḷum, nālaṭiyār tokainūlām; ēṉaiyavai taṉi nūlkaḷ. kaṭaik kaḻakattār pāṭiya pāṭṭukaḷilum nūlkaḷilum palavaṟṟait tokuttapōtu, pattuppāṭṭum eṭṭut tokaiyumākiya patiṉeṭṭuk koppākak kīḻkkaṇakkaiyum patiṉeṭ ṭākat tokuttirukkiṉṟaṉar. it tokai irupuṟavoppup (Symmetry) paṟṟiyatu.
aḵteṅṅaṉa miruppiṉum, puṟanāṉūṟu oru tokainūl allatu taṉippāṭaṟṟiraṭṭē. puṟapporuḷaip paṟṟiya nāṉūṟu ceyyuḷkaḷaik koṇṭatu puṟanāṉūṟu. iṉṉā nāṟpatu iṉiyavai nāṟpatu mutaliya nāṟpatu pākkaḷaik koṇṭa paṉuvalkaḷaiyum, naṟṟiṇai nāṉūṟu, kuṟuntokai nāṉūṟu, nālaṭi nāṉūṟu, paḻamoḻi nāṉūṟu mutaliya nāṉūṟu pākkaḷaik koṇṭa paṉuvalkaḷaiyum, nālāyirakkōvai nālāyirat tivyappirapantam (teyvappaṉuval) mutaliya nālāyiram pākkaḷaik koṇṭa paṉuvalkaḷaiyum nōkkumiṭattu, nāleṉṉum eṇ ētō voru kāraṇampaṟṟi nūṟceyyuṭ ṭokaikkuc ciṟantatākak koḷḷap paṭṭateṉpatu terikiṉṟatu. atu nālvakaip pāvō nālvakaik kulamō paṟṟiyirukkalām.
4. naṭai
paṇṭait tamiḻnūlkaḷellām taṉittamiḻ eṉṉum potuk koḷkaikkēṟpa, puṟanāṉūṟum taṉit tamiḻē. āṅkāṅku iraṇṭoru vaṭacoṟkaḷ aruki vantiruppatiṉāl, puṟanāṉūṟṟiṉ taṉittamiḻt taṉmai kuṉṟiviṭātu. in nūlil vantirukkum vaṭacoṟkaḷellām irupatu allatu irupaittaintē. ivaṟṟuḷḷum orucila teṉ collō vaṭacollō eṉa aiyuṟaṟkuriyaṉa.
iraṇṭoru vaṭacoṟkaḷ iṅkumaṅkumāka aruki vantiruppatu koṇṭu, puṟanāṉūṟu muḻumaiyuṅ kalapput tamiḻeṉpārāyiṉ, piṟamoḻic col kalavāta moḻiyē ulakattilillai yeṉṟum, āriyattilum cuṭṭum viṉāvum mūviṭap peyarum pōṉṟa aṭippaṭaic coṟka ḷellām vērnilaiyil tamiḻā yiruttaliṉ, tamiḻ taṉittu vaḻaṅkiṉum āriyam taṉittu vaḻaṅkal kūṭāmai yeṉṟum kūṟi viṭukka.
puṟanāṉūṟṟuc ceyyuḷkaḷiṟ cila citaintuṅ kuṟaintu miruppatāl, in nūṟ ceyyuḷkaḷiṉ aṭicciṟumai perumaiyum coṟṟokaiyum aṟivataṟkillai. āyiṉum, itupōtuḷḷapaṭi aṭiyuñ collum eṇṇip pārppiṉ, oru ceyyuḷiṉ carācari aṭittokai patiṉeṭ ṭeṉpatum, ōr aṭiyiṉ carācaric coṟṟokai āṟeṉpatum teriya varum. ākavē, oru ceyyuḷiṉ carācaric coṟṟokai ēṟattāḻa nūṟeṉpatu peṟappaṭum.
5.	puṟanāṉūṟṟāl viḷakkappaṭum colliyal (Etymological) neṟimuṟaikaḷum uṇmaikaḷum
1.	cuṭṭaṭic coṟkaḷ mutaṟkālattil uyir mutala.
	e-ṭu : ēm-ēmam-cēmam = kāval, pātukāval (1, 3). cēmavaccu (102). cēmam-kṣēma (va.). o.nō : ēṇ-cēṇ.
(2)	cuṭṭuviṉāc coṟkaḷ mutaṟkālattil neṭiṉ mutala.
	e-ṭu : āṅku = acai (2), appaṭi (24) : atupōla (35, 106).
		īṅkaṉam = ivvāṟu (208), ītu (208).
		ūṅku = muṉ (79, 88, 141).
		ēṉōr = yāvar (342), yāṅkaṉam = evvāṟu (8,49).
		yāṅku = eppaṭi (191, 245)
(3)	cuṭṭeḻuttukaḷ mutaṟkālattil peyarc collāyumiruntaṉa.
	e-ṭu. : attaka = ataṟkuttaka (10).
(4)	olikkuṟippallāc coṟkaḷellām mutaṟkālattil ōracaiya.
	e-ṭu : ār = uṇ (24), ārakkāl (283), ātti (338),
		āl = āṭu (116).
		uṇ (160), uḷ = ūkkam (396), ūm = ūmai (28) ūr (83).
(5)	mutaṟkālattil oru vaṭivē pala colvakaikkup potuvāyiruntatu.
	e-ṭu :	cey = ceykai (199), pari = celavu (14, 146), aṟivārā = aṟiyavārā (92), nakutakkaṉar = cirikkattakkār (72), iṉaikūu = varuntik kūppiṭum kūppīṭu (44), iṉaiyum kūvu eṉka. vaṇaṅkiṟai = vaḷainta muṉkai (32).
(6)	ellāc collum poruḷkuṟit taṉavē (tol.).
	e-ṭu :	cuḷ-cuṭu-cuṭar, kaḷ-kaṇ, eyyil-eyil, kaṇ-kaṇṇi, ol-oṉ-oṉṉār, maṟu-maṟai.
		kuḷ-kuṭa-kuṭai, vaḷ-vari-varicai, aṇ-aṭu-aṭar-aṭavi, iru-eru-erumai, a-aṉ-aṉai, māṟu-māṟē, iru-iṉu-eṉu-ēṉai-yāṉai, pul-puḷ-puṇar,	vē-vēku-vēkam, vēku-vekuḷ-vekuḷi, iṟu-iṟai-iṟaiñcu, nal-nalku, oḷ-oḷi-oḷir-oḷiṟu, maru-mār-mārpu, kaḷ-kaḷi-kaḷiṟu, iru-irā-iravu, koḷ-koṭu-koṭi, koḷ-koṇpu-kompu, kuṭi-kuṭumpu-kuṭumpi-kuṭumi, pul-poḷ-pōḻ-pōḻtu-poḻutu-poḻutam,	pul-pol-poru-porun-poruntu.
		poru-poruḷ, vai-vacai, kaḷ-karu-karum-karumpu, kil-kiḷ-kēḷ-kēṇ-kēṇi, kēḷ-kēḻal, paṇ-pāṇ-pāṇaṉ, kiḷ-kēḷ, i-iṉ-iṉi, puri-puricai, vaḷ-vāḷ-vāḷai, tī-tīmai, pul-puṉmai, vaḷ-vaṭku-vaṭkār.
acainilaikaḷ :	attai = atai, ittai = itai, mātō = peṇṇē, maṉṉō = aracē, mati = aṟivākum, aḷavā kum, pōtum, ōr = uṇar, ōrum - uṇarum, āyum. kuraittu = perumaiyuṭaiyatu. amma = ammaiyē. til = uḷḷam (?) kol = ō (?) ō-vō-kō-kōl-kol.
(7)	moḻipporuṭ kāraṇam viḻippat tōṉṟā (tol.)
	i-ḷ :	coṟkaḷiṉ vērpporuḷ pārtta allatu kēṭṭa maṭṭil tōṉṟā e-tu.
	in nūṟpāviṟkup palkalaikkaḻaka akarātip patippāḷar, Tolkappiyar says that the origin of words beyond ascertainment” eṉṟu viḷakkaṅ kūṟiyiruppatu mikamika iraṅkattakkatu.
(8)	kumarināṭṭuc coṟkaḷ pala iṟantupaṭṭaṉa. ataṉāl vēr maṟainta coṟkaḷ vērpporuḷ tāṅki nillā.
(9)	oru poruṭkup palakāraṇampaṟṟip pala col tōṉṟum.
	e-ṭu :	kaṭal = vaḷaintatu, periyatu; munnīr = mūṉṟu nīrai yuṭaiyatu; yāṉai = kariyatu. kaimmāṉ = kaiyai yuṭaiya vilaṅku. vēḻam = karumpaiyum mūṅkilaiyum tiṉpatu.
		(kaḻai = mūṅkil (158), kaḻaikkarumpu (137), kaḻaitiṉ yāṉaiyār - oru pulavar (204)).
(10)	orē kāraṇampaṟṟip pala poruṭku allatu karuttukkup palvēṟu col tōṉṟum.
	e-ṭu. :	iṟu - iṟaṅkutal = vaḷaital (98), iṟaiñcuka = vaṇaṅkuka (6).
		am-ampu = vāṇam (vaḷaivatu), āmpi = kāḷāṉ.
		kuḷ-kuṭai = vaḷainta kavikai, koṭumai = vaḷaivu (39).
		tiri - tiriyāc cuṟṟam (2), tiruki = kuṟuki (27), tiruki-tikiri (32), puri-puri = muṟukku (135), puricai = matil (17), vaḷ-vaḷai, vaṇar-vaḷaivu (455), vaṇaṅku = vaḷainta (32), muḷ-muṭam (307).
(11)	oru karuttiṉiṉṟu vēṟu karuttut tōṉṟum.
	e-ṭu :	kamaḻa - parakka (50), vīciya - citaṟiya (23),
	kuṟumpu = ciṟṟaraṇ, kuṟumparacar, kuṟumparacar ceyyum tīmai.
	cemmai = civappu, nērmai.
(12)	parupporuṭ karuttiṉiṉṟu nuṇporuṭ karuttum, nuṇporuṭ karuttiṉiṉṟu parupporuṭ karuttun tōṉṟum.
	e-ṭu :	ataṉ talai = ataṟku mēlē (19),
		kavalai = kavartta vaḻi (3), maṉakkavalai.
		muḷ - muy-muyal-mayal-māl-mālai,
		tul-tulām=oppu, tarācu (39).
(13)	oruporuṭ coṟkaḷiṉiṉṟu otta karuttut tōṉṟalām.
	e-ṭu :	poru = poruntu, pōr cey; camam - oppu, pōr (14)
		uṟu = poruntu, uṟuvar = etirntavar (11),
		uṟaḻtal = māṟupaṭutal (22),
		uṭal = uṭaṉiruppatu. uṭalutal = pakaittal (25),
		uṭalunar = māṟupaṭuvōr,
		akal = viri, nīṅku; paṭar = viri, cel. acai = iyaṅku. varuntu ; cali = acai, varuntu, kaḷai.
(14)	poruḷ vēṟupaṭac col vēṟupaṭum.
	e-ṭu :	maṇ-maṇal. talaiyaṉ - talaivaṉ, vaḻipaṭu-vaḻippaṭu. paṭiya - paṭikka. tēyntāṉ - tēyttāṉ.
		ī-vī-vīḻ-viḻai-viḻā-viḻavu.
		vīḻ - viḻu - viḻum- viḻuppam. viḻumiya = ciṟanta.
		vīḻ - viḻu. vīḻ - vīḻtu - viḻutu.
		viḻu - viḻal. vī = viḻunta malar. kāṉa vūkiṉ kaḻaṉṟuku mutuvī (307). vī kamaḻ vīḻumpū nāṟukiṉṟa (36, urai)
		ÉG-ÉF-ÉFâ-É¡Uâ (t.), o. nō, toḻu-toku.
		vītal = viḻutal, muṭital, iṟattal.
		viku + tam = vikutam - vikitam = viḻukkāṭu.
		vikutam = vītam.
		vīḻ - vīḷ - viḷ - virum - virumpu.
		virum - viruntu = virumpiyiṭum uṇavu, virumpi yūṭṭappaṭum puttāḷ, putumai,
		viḷ - veḷ - vemmai - veyyōṉ.
		vēḷ - vēṇ - vēṇṭu - vēṇṭum - vēṇṭām - vēṇṭā.
		vēḷ - vēṭṭam - vēṭṭai - vēṭṭuvaṉ - vēṭuvaṉ - vēṭaṉ - vēṭu,
		vēḷ - vēṭkai,
		vēḷ - vēḷvi, vēḷāṇmai, vēḷāḷaṉ, vēḷāṉ, vēḷ, vēḷam,
		veḷ-peḷ-peṇ-pēṇ-pēṭu, pēṭaṉ, pēṭi

(15)	palvēṟu coltirintu oruvaṭivu koḷḷalām.
		vāḷ = oḷi (196). vāḷ = vāṭpaṭai (4),
		vil-viḷ-vāḷ = oḷi. vāḷ = veṇmai (6) tūymai (1). pāl = veḷḷamutam. pal = veṇmuṟuval.

(17)	tamiḻ eḻuttukaḷuḷ ṟa ṉa pintiyavai.
		oḷir - oḷiṟu. muri - muṟi. vāḻum - vāḻumar-vāḻunar-vāḻnar (9). porunaṉ (58) poruna (2,17). aṭunai (36).
		ṟa ṉa arivari meyvaricaiyil valliṉattōṭu cērkkap paṭāmal iṟutiyil vaikkappaṭṭiruttalai nōkkuka.
(18)	toḻiṟpeyar pakutiyātalumuṇṭu.
	e-ṭu :	naccu + ai - nacai, nacai vēṭkai = nacciya viruppam (3).
(19)	kaṭaik kaḻakak kālattilēyē nuṇporuṭ coṟkaḷaip parupporuḷil vaḻaṅkat toṭaṅkiviṭṭaṉar.
	e-ṭu :	ciṟu tuṉi (366). peruma vuraitticiṉ (167).
(20)	cila coṟkaḷum col vaṭivaṅkaḷum ceyyuḷil allatu cila paṉuvalkaḷil iṭam peṟā.

	ceytāl, āṉāl - col vaṭivam.
(21)	oruporuṭ pala coṟkaḷ nuṇporuḷ vēṟupāṭṭiṉa.
	e-ṭu :	kaṇṇi = kaṇ kaṇṇāyp pūvait toṭuttut talaiyiṟ cūṭum toṭai (I).
		tār = mārpilaṇiyum kaṭṭi mālai (I)
		kōtai = pūmālaip potup peyar (48).
		toṭalai = taḻaimālai (271).
		āram = muttumālai (19).
		mālai - mālaip potuppeyar (95).
(22)	oru poruṭkut takunta collai vaḻaṅka vēṇṭum.
	e-ṭu :	ñāyiṟu ... kuṭakaṭaṟ kuḷikkum (2).
		anti yantaṇar aruṅkaṭa ṉiṟukkum (2).
(23) tamiḻ peruñ colvaḷattatu.
		kal, aṟai, kuṉṟu, kuṉṟam, poṟai, malai - malaip peyarkaḷ.
		cāral, kavāṉ, cimai, kuvaṭu - kōṭu, aṭukkam - malaippakutip peyarkaḷ.
(24)	coṟporuḷ vaḻakkum paṟṟiyatu.
	e-ṭu :	nāṟṟam = naṟumaṇam (70).
		cilvaḷai viṟali (103), ciṉṉīr (154).
(25)	vaḻakkaṟṟa collē aruñcollākum.
	e-ṭu :	yāḻ (ta), - vīṇai. tiṅkaḷ (ka) = mātam, naku, navvu (te.) = ciri.
(26)	iṉapporuḷkaḷ potuppeyaruṅ koḷḷum.
	e-ṭu :	tāḻai - teṅku (17).
(27)	ayaṟcol vaḻaṅkutal kūṭātu : vaḻaṅka nēriṉ tamiḻukkēṟpat tirittal vēṇṭum.
	e-ṭu :	vijaya (va.) - vicaya (ta.).
(28)	coṟkaḷiṉ muntu vaṭivam pārttup poruḷ koḷḷal vēṇṭum.
	e-ṭu :	pañilam - paiññilam - painnilam = paṭaittokuti (62).
		o.nō. painniṇam = paiññiṉam (177).
		viḻavu - viḻā - viḻai = virumpu.
6.	puṟanāṉūṟṟāl viḷakkappaṭum moḻinūl (Philological) neṟimuṟaikaḷum uṇmaikaḷum
(1)	tamiḻ tōṉṟiyatu kumarināṭē.
	munnīr viḻavi ṉeṭiyōṉ
	naṉṉīrp paḵṟuḷi maṇaliṉum palavē. (9).
(2)	tamiḻ vaṭamoḻikku muntiyatu.
	maṉpatai - makkaṭ kūṭṭam (210) maṉ - man (Lit.) the thinking animal. A.S. mann-root man to think, cognate with Ger. and Goth, man, Ice. madhr. (for mannar) Ch.E.D. - manu(va.).
	kāṇ - A.S. cunnan, to know, E. cunning, knowing, con, to study, carefully, A.S. cnawan, Ice. kna-E. know L. gnosco. Skt. jna. kāṭci ñāṉam.
	viḻi - L video (visum), to see, E vide, Skt. - vit, vētam : vaityam.
(3)	tamiḻukku vaṭamoḻit tuṇai vēṇṭuvatiṉṟu.
			vaṭamoḻi	tamiḻ	vaṭamoḻi	tamiḻ
	e-ṭu:	cāpam	vil	ūci (utīci)	vaṭakku
			nēmi	tikiri	pāci (pirāci)	kiḻakku
			vētam	maṟai	cātam	cōṟu
	evvakaiyiṉum tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukkuḷ talaiciṟappap paṇpaṭuttappeṟṟa tamiḻ, vēṇṭumāyiṉ, vaṭamoḻit toṭarpai aṟavē vilakkuvatuṭaṉ, taṉittu vaḻaṅkutal maṭṭumaṉṟu, ataṉ utaviyiṉṟit taḻaittōṅkutalum iyalum (tirāviṭa oppiyal ilakkaṇam : muṉṉurai. pa. 50).
(4)	vaṭamoḻiyil oru pakuti tamiḻē.
	ulakam, kalai, kālam, cāyai, māyai, mīṉam, vaḷaiyam, paṭimai mutaliya eṇṇiṟanta tamiḻccoṟkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyiṟ ceṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.

(7)	otta kāraṇattāl pala moḻiyil coṟkaḷ tōṉṟalām.
e-ṭu :	pari - kutirai (vēkamāy ōṭuvatu), E. courser, a runner
	L. curro, to run.
	paḻam - paḻaṉ - payaṉ. E. fruit = effect, result.
	kumarippaṭai, kaṉṉippōr. E. maiden = new, first, 			virgin, undefiled, fresh, unpolluted.
	toḷ - tōṇi (=tōṇṭappaṭṭatu). E. dug out a boat 		made by hollowing out the trunk of a tree.
	cey - ceyyuḷ. E. poem. Lit. anything made L. poema. 		Gk. poiema - poieo, to do or make.
	olittal = taḻaittal (50). E. tonic, “a medicine which 		gives tone or vigour to the system.”

ī-iccu (te.). tā - L. do, to give; donum, a gift, tāṉa(va.).
(9) tamiḻ iyaṟkai moḻi.
āriyamoḻikaḷum tamiḻallāta tirāviṭa moḻikaḷum tamiḻukku mikamikap piṟpaṭṭavaiyāyiruntum, peritum vaṭivu tirintum vērp poruḷiḻantum paṉṉilaiyaṭaintum muṉṉilaikkup piṉṉilai vēṟupaṭṭumuḷḷaṉa. tamiḻō talaikkaḻakak kālattiliruntu ituvarai perumpālun tiriyāmal, ki.mu. 2000 pōl iyaṟṟappaṭṭa tol kāppiyattilum ki.pi. 2ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉatāṉa puṟanāṉūṟṟilum iṟṟait tūya ilakkiya naṭaiyilum collamaitiyum coṟṟoṭaramaippum perumpālum ottatāy, ciṟitu kaṟṟārkkum ōraḷavu viḷaṅkuvatāy vērpporuḷ tāṅkik koṇṭuḷḷatu.
(10) kaṭaikkaḻakat tamiḻ taṉittamiḻē.
puṟanāṉūṟṟiṟ cila ceyyuḷkaḷil iraṇṭoru vaṭacoṟkaḷē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. pala ceyyuḷkaḷil vaṭacollēyillai.
e-ṭu : yātu mūrē, (192)
ākaiyāl, nūliṉ mottac coṟkaḷil nūṟṟukkoṉṟukūṭa vaṭacollillaiyeṉpatu teḷivu.
(11) kaṭaik kaḻakak kālattilēyē vaṭacoṟ kaḷaikaḷ muḷaittut tamiḻccoṟ payirkaḷaik keṭukkat toṭaṅkiviṭṭaṉa.
añcaṉam (mai), āyuḷ (vāḻnāḷ), āvuti (avi), intirar (vēntar), imayam (paṉimalai), utīci - ūci (vaṭakku), entiram (poṟi, maṇai, cūḻcciyam), kalavam (pīli, tōkai) kavuri (māyōḷ, malaimakaḷ), cāpam (vil), tarumam (aṟam), tūmam (naṟumpukai, vāl veḷḷi, viṇvīḻkoḷḷi), niccam (nāḷum, eṉṟum), nirayam (aḷaṟu), nēmi (āḻi, tikiri), paṅkuṉi (uttaram), pañcavar (aivar, pāṇṭavar), pirāci - pāci (kiḻakku), yūpam (kantu, vēḷvittūṇ), vicayam (koṟṟam, veṟṟi) vētam (maṟai).
ivaiyē puṟanāṉūṟṟil vantuḷḷa teḷivāṉa vaṭacoṟkaḷ. ivai tamiḻukku iṉṟiyamaiyātaṉavō ciṟappuc ceyvaṉavō alla. ivai, āriyar vaṭamoḻi tēvamoḻi yeṉṟum camayamoḻi eṉṟum ēmāṟṟiya piṉ, camaiyam vāykkum pōtellām, tamiḻarkkut teriyātavāṟu, tamiḻc coṟkaḷai vaḻakku vīḻttumporuṭṭu ovvoṉṟāyt tamiḻiṟ pukuttiyavai. ivaṟṟāl tamiḻukkup palavakaiyilum tīmaiyēyaṉṟi naṉmaiyillai. at tīmaikaḷāvaṉa :
(1) teṉcol maṟaivu
tamiḻccoṟkaṭkup patilāka vaṭacoṟkaḷai vaḻaṅkavē, muṉṉavai mutalāvatu vaḻakkiṟantaṉa; piṉṉar, paṇṭaik kālattil virivāṉa akarātikaḷ tokukkappaṭāmaiyālum, tolkāppiyam oḻinta toṉṉūlkaḷellām iṟantupaṭṭamaiyālum, tamiḻakattiṉ perum pakutiyāṉa kumarināṭṭaik kaṭal koṇṭamaiyālum, nāḷaṭaivil mīṭpaṟa maṟaintoḻintaṉa.
(2) teṉcol vaḻakkaḻivu
vaṭacol vaḻakkālum, ataṉāl tamiḻar tamiḻuṇarcci yaṟṟamai yālum,
aṭar (kampiyākac cey), aṭukkam (pakkamalai), aṇaṅku (teyvap peṇ, mōkiṉi), aṇal (tāṭi), alkupatam (iṭṭu vaittuṇṇum uṇavu), aḻuvam (mēṟparappu), aḷitu (pāvam), aṟattuṟaiyampi, āṇṭakai (pahatūr), iyam (vāttiyam), iyavar (vāttiyakkārar), iḷaiyaṉ (javāṉ), uraṟu (karcci), ōvam (cittiram), uvakaikkaluḻcci (āṉantak kaṇṇīr), eruvai (talaiveḷuttu uṭal civantirukkum paruntu), aiyavi (veṇciṟu kaṭuku), aimpaṭai (pañcāyutam), kaṇṇuṟai (mēlīṭu), kaṇṇōṭṭam (tāṭciṇiyam), kataḻvu (viraivu), kaṇiyaṉ (jōtiṭaṉ), kaḻuvāy (pirāyaccittam), kiḻār (perumpaṇṇaiyār), kuṇil (kuṟuntaṭi, latti), kurucil (pirapu), kōy (kaḷmukakkum kalam), cuval (tōḷ), cūḷ (āṇai), cemmal (nērmaiyāṉavaṉ), cevvi (takka camaiyam), tōṉṟal (araca makaṉ), navil (paṉmuṟai collip paḻaku), nuval (nuṭpamāyc col), neṭumoḻi (taṟpukaḻcci maṟaivurai), pañilam (Regiment), paṭaivīṭu (Cantonment), paṟantalai (pōrkkaḷam), pūṭkai (tenent, morale), porunaṉ (cippāy), maṉpatai (Humanity), matavali (Sando), paṉuval (pirapantam), maṟuku (vīti), maḻavar (vīrar), maḷḷar (vīrar), mātiram (ticai), yāṇar (putuvaruvāy), vañciṉam (capatam), vayavar (vīrar), valvil (vilvalaṉ), vampalaṉ (nilaiyillātavaṉ, iṭamāṟi), vēḷ (camīṉtār), vēḷāṇmai (upacāram), mutaliya eṇṇiṟanta tamiḻccoṟkaḷ vaḻakkoḻintaṉa.
(3) teṉcoṟ poruḷiḻappu
tamiḻccoṟkup patilāka vaṭacoṟkaḷai vaḻaṅkiyatāl cila tamiḻc coṟkaḷ poruḷiḻantu muḷḷaṉa.
e-ṭu : uyirmey.
poruḷkaḷellām uyir, mey, uyirmeyyeṉa mūṉṟā yaṭaṅkum. ivaṟṟaiyotta eḻuttukaḷum, uyir, mey, uyirmey eṉap (uvamaiyākupeyar) peṟṟaṉa. pirāṇi eṉṉum vaṭacol vaḻaṅkavē uyirmey eṉṉuñ col taṉ poruḷai yiḻantatu. pirāṇaṉai yuṭaiyatu pirāṇi. uyiraiyuṭaiya mey uyirmey.
(4) aiyaṟavu
vaṭacoṟ kalappāl, ēṉāti, kavacam, cakaṭu, tāṉai, tēyam, pāram, putalvar, vatuvai, vāttiyam (vāḻttiyam) mutaliya pala coṟkaḷ vaṭacollō teṉcollō eṉa moḻiyārāycciyāḷarum mayaṅkaṟ kiṭaṉāyuḷḷaṉa. vaṭacoṟ kalantirāviṭiṉ, tamiḻiluḷḷa collellām tamiḻccollēyeṉṟu koḷḷappaṭumaṉṟō!
(5) vaṭacoṟ pōṉmai
vaṭacoṟ kalappiṉāl, pala tūya teṉcoṟkaḷ, āriya moḻikaḷil ceṉṟu vaḻaṅkuvatu kāraṇamāka, allatu āriyac collōṭoli yottamai kāraṇamāka, āriya allatu vaṭacoṟkaḷāka ārāycci yillārkkut tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. im mayakkattait tīrkkumukamāka, mutalāvatu, mēlaiyāriyattil vaḻaṅkum puṟanāṉūṟṟut tamiḻccoṟkaḷai yeṭuttuk kāṭṭukiṉṟēṉ.
7. mēlaiyāriyat tamiḻccoṟkaḷ
amar - L. amo, to love. E. amateur, amiable, amicable; aḷam - E. alum, a mineral salt, L. alumen. iṭṭu - E. and Fr. et. ette, a dim. suffix ; uraṟu - E. roar, A.S. rurian, Ger. rohren; uruḷ - E. roll, role, L. rota, a wheel; uḷḷu - E. will, A.S. willa, Goth. vilja, Ger. wille, L. vola, to will Gk. boule, will (uḷḷam) ; el - Gk. helios, the sun ; ellā - E. all, A.S. eal, Cer, all,Gael. uile, W. oll ; kaṭai - E. gate, A.S. geat, a way, a gate ; karappu - Gk. kryptos, concealed, karai - E. cry, L. queri, to wail ; kalleṉa (kavi) - E call; A.S. ceallian, Ice. kalla ; kavaii - kava - E, have, L. capio to contain; kaṟaṅku - E. ring A.S. hringan, Ice, hringia; kāṇ - E. con, A.S. cunnan, to know ; kāl - L. column, kiḻi - E. cleave, A.S. cleofan, Gk. kliben; kiḷar - L. hiloris, cheerful; kiḷai (kēḷ) - E. kin, A.S. cyn, Ice, kyn ; kuppai - E. heap, A.S. heap. kū - E. coo ; kuḻu - E. clew, clue; kuḻumu L.glomus, E. glomerate, kuṟukku - E.cross, Fr. crois - L. curx. koṭukku - E. crook, W. crwg, a hook, Ice. krokr, Dut - kroke, a fold as wrinkle; kol - E. kill, quell, A.S. qwellan, to kill; camam - E. same, A.S. and Goth samana, L.similis, Cor. homos, Skt, samas; citai - coede, to kill ; cuṟavu - E. shark ; curuṅku - E. shrink, cēr - L. sero, to join ; cūḷ - E. swear, A.S. swerian, Dut. zweren, Ger. schworen; tā - L. do ; tīrmāṉam - L. terminus, a boundary ; tiṇ - E. dense. iṉi, piṟaku (back), pukai (smoke), paiyuḷ (pain); poṟi (Gk. pur. fire), vaṭṭi (vat) vēli (pale) mutaliya aiyuṟavāṉa coṟ pōlikaḷum, vāṭu (fade), vēṇṭu (want) mutaliya pōli yoppumaic coṟkaḷum ettuṇaiyō uḷaveṉṟaṟika.
āṅkilam paṇṭaikkālattē tamiḻoṭu kalantirukkumāyiṉ, mēṟ kāṭṭiya coṟkaḷellām āṅkilattiṉiṉṟu tamiḻukku vantavaiyākak karutappaṭumaṉṟō?
attōṭu, boy, nation mutaliya coṟkaḷkūṭa, paiyaṉ nāṭu mutaliya coṟkaḷākat tirintatākak koḷḷappaṭumaṉṟō! iṅṅaṉamē vaṭacoṟ pōl tōṉṟum teṉcoṟkaḷumeṉka.
8. vaṭacoṟ pōlikaḷ
antaṇaṉ :	am + taṇ + aṉ = antaṇaṉ. aḻakiya kuḷirnta aruḷaiyuṭaiyavaṉ eṉpatu poruḷ. ākavē, tuṟavi allatu muṉivaṉ peyarām.
antaṇa reṉpō raṟavōrmaṟ ṟevvuyirkkum
centaṇmai pūṇṭoḻuka lāṉ	(kuṟaḷ.)
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar. antaṇar, aracar, vaṇikar, vēḷāḷar eṉpatu paḻantamiḻp pakuppu.
iṉi, vētaṅkaḷiṉ antattai aṇavuvōṉ eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟiṉum, antaṇaṉ eṉpatu tamiḻccollē. aṇavu = poruntu. aṇ-aṇa-aṇavu. antam kīḻk kāṇka.
anti : al - antu - antam - antaram = muṭivu. al - alku = muṭi, aḻi. al - aṟu. antu - anti = pakaṟ kālattiṉ muṭivu. neṭiṟ curaṅkaḷai antarakkōl eṉpatu icaittamiḻ vaḻakku.
amaraṉ : a + maraṉ = amaraṉ. maṭi - mari - maraṉ. al - a, etirmaṟai muṉṉoṭṭu muṭi - maṭi. iṉi, amariṉāl tēvarulakai yaṭaintavaṉ eṉiṉum poruntum.
amayam: camai + am = camaiyam = oṉṟaṟkuc camainta vēḷai, vēḷai.  camaiyam - camayam = āṉmā vīṭṭaiyaṭaitaṟkuc camaiyum neṟi. camai - amai. amai + am = amaiyam - amayam. camaital = pakkuvamātal. camaittal = cōṟṟaip pakkuvamākkal. amai - amaivu, amaippu, amaiti.
amiḻtu : avi - aviḻ - aviḻtu - aviḻtam = cōṟu. cōṟṟup pāl, pāl, maruntu. (aṉṉappāl, irumaruntu eṉṉum vaḻakku kaḷai nōkkuka) aviḻtu - amiḻtu - amutu. aviḻtam - amiḻtam - amutam. amutu = cōṟu, amutam = cōṟu, pāl, nīr.
avalam : a + valam = avalam. a, etirmaṟai muṉṉoṭṭu. val - valam = valimai. avalam = tuṉpuṟutaṟ kētuvāṉa valimaiyaṟṟa nilai, tuṉpam.
aracu : ar - ari - aram - aracu. aram - araṉ = aḻippavaṉ, tēvaṉ, civaṉ. aramakaḷir = tēva makaḷir, aracu + aṉ = aracaṉ = tēvaṉaip pōṉṟavaṉ, talaivaṉ. aracaṉ - araicaṉ - araiyaṉ - araiyam. aracu - araicu. araiyaṉ - rāyaṉ. E.roy, aracu E. arch; aracaṉ - Gk. archon. aracu = aracu pōṉṟa maram; pūvaracu = pūvaiyuṭaiya aracu, āṟṟaracu = āṟṟaṅkaraiyaracu.
avi :	 avi = avikkappaṭṭa vuṇavu, uṇavu.
avai : avi - avir = viḷaṅku. avi - avai = kaṟṟaṭaṅkiyōr allatu aṟivāl viḷaṅkuvōr kuḻu. o.nō : E. galaxy = the Milky-Way, any splendid assemblage. avai + am = avaiyam.
āyutam : āy = kaḷai, oṭi, veṭṭu, āy + tam = āytam - āyutam = veṭṭum karuvi, karuvi, paṭai.
ilakkam (kuṟi) : el - ilaku - ilakku. ilakkam = viḷakkam, viḷaṅkum iṭam, iṭam, kuṟi, viḷakkattiṟ keṭuttuk kāṭṭu. ellē yilakkam eṉpatu tolkāppiyam. ilakku + iyam = ilakkiyam = eṭuttuk kāṭṭu, kuṟi. cf. E. illustrate-L. illustratum, to light up. nūṟ ṟokutiyaik kuṟikkum ilakkiyam eṉṉuñ col vēṟu. aḵtu ilakku (= eḻutu) eṉpataṉaṭiyāyp piṟantatu.
urakam : ura = poruntu, taḻuvu, uram = mārpu. o.nō ; maru - marumam - mār - mārpu, maruvu = kala, taḻuvu. uram - urakam = mārpiṉāl ūrvatu, pāmpu.
uru :	uru = (vi), tōṉṟu. (pe.) tōṟṟam, vaṭivam, vaṭivattaiyuṭaiya oru poruḷ, uṭampu, oru vaṭivattiṟkuḷ eḻutappaṭum mantiram. mantiram pōṉṟa pāṭṭu. uru - uruvu - uruvam. uruvu - urupu = vēṟṟumai vaṭivam. uruppaṭi = oru taṉipporuḷ. uruppaṭu, urukkulai mutaliya nūṟṟuk kaṇakkāṉa kūṭṭuc coṟkaḷ toṉṟu toṭṭu ulaka vaḻakkil vaḻaṅkutalaiyum, urupu eṉpatu ōr ilakkaṇak kuṟiyīṭāy iruttalaiyum nōkkuka.
ulakam : cuḷ - cul - culam - culavu - ulavu = vaḷai, cuṟṟu. culam - ulam - ulaku - ulakam = vaṭṭamā yiruppatu allatu cuḻalvatu allatu katiravaṉaic cuṟṟi varuvatu. ulam varuvōr (puṟam, 51) = cuḻalvōr. ulā = nakarai valamākac cuṟṟi varutal, o.nō. : koḷ - kōḷ = Planet. koḷ = vaḷai. cuṟṟu, koṭpu = cuṟṟukai. koḷ - koṭu - kōṭu - kōṇu.
ēmam : ē-ēm-ēmam = pātukāppu, iṉpam. ē = uyarcci, ``ē peṟṟākum'', eṉpatu tolkāppiyam. vilaṅkukaḷālum pakaivarālum veḷḷat tālum tuṉpam nērumpōtu, uyarnta iṭam pātukāppiṟkētu vāṉatu. pātukāppāl iṉpamuṇṭākum. ēmam - cēmam, o.nō : ēṇ - cēṇ. cēmavaccu (102).
ēṉāti : ēṉai (yāṉai) + ari = ēṉāri - ēṉāti = yāṉaiyaik koṉṟavaṉ. o.nō : kōṭu + ari = kōṭari. parukkai - patukkai.
kaṭumpu : kuṭam - kuṭi - kuṭumpu - kaṭumpu. kuṭumpu - kuṭumpam. kuṭi = uṭa ṉuḷḷatu, uṭampu pōṉṟa vīṭu, vīṭṭu vāḻkkai, vīṭṭiluḷḷa maṉaivi, maṉaivi makkaḷākiya kuṭumpam, kuṭumpa marapu, kulam, oru kulam vāḻiṭam, ūr. kaṭumpu = kuṭumpam, cuṟṟam.
kaṇaṉ : kala - kaḷa - kaḷam - kaṇam - kaṇaṉ = kūṭṭam.
kantaṉ : kal - kantu - kantam - kantaṉ = āṉmākkaṭkuk kaṟṟūṇ pōṟ paṟṟukkōṭāṉavaṉ, murukaṉ. kantaḻi = paṟṟukkōṭaṟṟa kaṭavuḷ.
kantapurāṇattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷapaṭi, kantaṉ imayamalaic caravaṇap poykaiyiṟ piṟantavaṉallaṉ. imayamalai tōṉṟiyataṟku muṉpē kumarināṭṭil kuṟiñcit teyvamāka vaṇaṅkappaṭṭavaṉ. mutaṟkālattil murukaṉuñ civaṉum oruvaṉē. tiṇaimayakka mēṟpaṭṭapiṉ, murukaṉ muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷākavum vaṇaṅkappaṭṭāṉ.
kammiyaṉ :	kaṭu-karu. kaṭu-kaṭi-kari. kaṭuttal = mikutal. karuttal = tōṉṟutal. karittal = mikutal. mikutaṟ karuttiṉiṉṟum tōṉṟaṟ karuttiṉiṉṟum ceytaṟ karuttut tōṉṟiyatākat terikiṉṟatu. o.nō. : E. make, from L. magnus, great. alam + kari = alaṅkari = aḻaku cey. karu kari eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷ ceytaṟ poruḷil vaḻaṅkiya nūlkaḷ iṟantupaṭṭaṉa. paṇṭaikkālattil virivāṉa akarātikaḷ eḻutappaṭāmaiyāl, kumarināṭṭuc coṟkaḷil palavaṟṟaik kāṭṭuvataṟku oru vaḻiyumillai. kari + aṇam = karaṇam = ceykai, ceyyum caṭaṅku, maṇaccaṭaṅku, ceyyuṅkaruvi. o.nō : vari + aṇam = varaṇam. karu + vi = karuvi. karu + mam = karumam. o.nō : paru + mam = parumam. karaṇam - kāraṇam. kari + iyam = kariyam - kāriyam. o.nō : kaṇṭam - kāṇṭam. karu - kru (va.). o.nō : tura - tru (va.). karumam = ceykai, toḻil, viṉaip payaṉ, ceyappaṭuporuḷ. karumam eṉpatu mutalāvatu uḻavut toḻilaiyum, piṉpu kaittoḻilaiyuṅ kuṟittatu. karumam - kammam - kam. kammavār - teluṅkuḻavar. kamakkāraṉ - uḻavaṉ. kammi = toḻilāḷi. kammāḷaṉ = aivakaik kollaruḷ oruvaṉ. kammiyam = kaittoḻil, kammāḷat toḻil, kammiyanūl = ciṟpanūl. kammiyaṉ = toḻilāḷi, kammāḷaṉ, neypavaṉ. karumakaṉ - karumāṉ - karumaṉ = kollaṉ. karumakaṉ eṉṉum peyaril karu eṉpatu toḻilaiyāvatu irumpaiyāvatu kuṟikkum.

āriyampōl ulakavaḻak kaḻintoḻintu citaintāyuṉ

eṉṟu iraṅkavē nērum.
